```
1	// 光照引擎：天空光列缓存 + 区域内 BFS 三通道衰减传播。
2	// 只维护「可视区 + 边距」的区域光照，脏时或相机跨阈值时重算。
3	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
4	import { lightIsOn } from '../world/wiring/devices';
5	import { LIGHT_AIR_COST, SKY_LIGHT, TILE } from '../core/constants';
6	import type { World } from '../world/World';
7	
8	const MARGIN = 26; // 区域外扩（tile），需 ≥ 光源最大传播距离（255/15 ≈ 17）
9	const MOVE_THRESHOLD = 2; // 相机中心移动多少 tile 触发重算
10	
11	export class LightingEngine {
12	  world: World;
13	  skyFirstSolid: Int32Array; // 每列第一个不透光 tile 的 y（-1 = 全通）
14	  // 区域
15	  rx = 0; ry = 0; rw = 0; rh = 0;
16	  r: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
17	  g: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
18	  b: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(0);
19	  dirty = true;
20	  liquidDirty = false; // 仅液体变化（水流频繁触发，重算限频 20Hz）；模拟器直写数组时由 Game 置位
21	  private tick = 0;
22	  private lastCamCX = -9999; private lastCamCY = -9999;
23	
24	  constructor(world: World) {
25	    this.world = world;
26	    this.skyFirstSolid = new Int32Array(world.w).fill(-1);
27	    this.rebuildSkyColumns();
28	    world.store.onTileChanged((x, y) => {
29	      this.rebuildSkyColumn(x);
30	      this.dirty = true;
31	    });
32	    // 液体变化只影响光衰减，不动天空列；频繁水流时避免每 tick 重建列缓存
33	    world.store.onLiquidChanged(() => {
34	      this.liquidDirty = true;
35	    });
36	  }
37	
38	  private blocksSky(type: number, wall: number): boolean {
39	    if (type !== 0) {
40	      const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
41	      if (d && d.opacity >= 0.9) return true;
42	    }
43	    return wall !== 0; // 有背景墙即视为地下（不透天空光）
44	  }
45	
46	  rebuildSkyColumn(x: number) {
47	    const st = this.world.store;
48	    let y = 0;
49	    for (; y < st.h; y++) {
50	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
51	      if (this.blocksSky(st.type[i], st.wall[i])) break;
52	    }
53	    this.skyFirstSolid[x] = y >= st.h ? -1 : y;
54	  }
55	
56	  rebuildSkyColumns() {
57	    for (let x = 0; x < this.world.w; x++) this.rebuildSkyColumn(x);
58	  }
59	
60	  ensureRegion(camCX: number, camCY: number, viewW: number, viewH: number) {
61	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camCX / TILE - viewW / 2) - MARGIN);
62	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camCY / TILE - viewH / 2) - MARGIN);
63	    const x1 = Math.min(this.world.w - 1, Math.ceil(camCX / TILE + viewW / 2) + MARGIN);
64	    const y1 = Math.min(this.world.h - 1, Math.ceil(camCY / TILE + viewH / 2) + MARGIN);
65	    const rw = x1 - x0 + 1, rh = y1 - y0 + 1;
66	    const moved = Math.abs(camCX - this.lastCamCX) > TILE * MOVE_THRESHOLD || Math.abs(camCY - this.lastCamCY) > TILE * MOVE_THRESHOLD;
67	    const resized = rw !== this.rw || rh !== this.rh;
68	    if (moved || resized) {
69	      // 关键：区域原点只和数据重算一起原子移动。
70	      // 否则原点跟人走、数据不动 → 光影相对世界平移（"黑影跟着飘"）。
71	      this.lastCamCX = camCX; this.lastCamCY = camCY;
72	      this.rx = x0; this.ry = y0;
73	      if (resized) {
74	        this.rw = rw; this.rh = rh;
75	        this.r = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
76	        this.g = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
77	        this.b = new Uint8Array(rw * rh);
78	      }
79	      this.dirty = true;
80	    }
81	  }
82	
83	  /** 取某 tile 的光照（区域外返回天空光近似） */
84	  lightAt(tx: number, ty: number, dayFactor: number): [number, number, number] {
85	    if (tx < this.rx || ty < this.ry || tx >= this.rx + this.rw || ty >= this.ry + this.rh) {
86	      const v = Math.round(SKY_LIGHT * dayFactor);
87	      return [v, v, v];
88	    }
89	    const i = (ty - this.ry) * this.rw + (tx - this.rx);
90	    return [this.r[i], this.g[i], this.b[i]];
91	  }
92	
93	  /** 手持光源（如火把），每帧由 Game 更新；值变化时置脏 */
94	  heldLight: { tx: number; ty: number } | null = null;
95	
96	  /** 每帧调用：脏则重算。液体引起的重算最多 20Hz（水流时每 tick 触发，全量 BFS 限频防卡顿） */
97	  compute(dayFactor: number) {
98	    if (this.dirty) {
99	      // tile/墙变化：立即重算
100	    } else if (this.liquidDirty) {
101	      this.tick++;
102	      if (this.tick % 3 !== 0) return; // 60fps → 20Hz
103	    } else {
104	      return;
105	    }
106	    if (this.rw === 0) return;
107	    this.dirty = false;
108	    this.liquidDirty = false;
109	    const { world, rx, ry, rw, rh } = this;
110	    const st = world.store;
111	    const r = this.r, g = this.g, b = this.b;
112	    r.fill(0); g.fill(0); b.fill(0);
113	
114	    // 种子队列（存区域索引，颜色按位打包在三个平面）
115	    const queue = new Int32Array(rw * rh * 4);
116	    let qh = 0, qt = 0;
117	    const push = (i: number) => { if (qt < queue.length) queue[qt++] = i; };
118	
119	    const skyV = Math.round(SKY_LIGHT * dayFactor);
120	    // 夜间月光偏蓝（红/绿压低、蓝抬高，按夜晚程度过渡）——
121	    // 避免夜间地表是一片死白的灰黑，火把暖光与月光冷色形成自然对比
122	    const nightK = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (1 - dayFactor) / 0.68)); // 0 白天 → 1 深夜
123	    const skyR = Math.round(skyV * (1 - 0.22 * nightK));
124	    const skyG = Math.round(skyV * (1 - 0.10 * nightK));
125	    const skyB = Math.min(255, Math.round(skyV * (1 + 0.18 * nightK)));
126	    for (let ly = 0; ly < rh; ly++) {
127	      for (let lx = 0; lx < rw; lx++) {
128	        const tx = rx + lx, ty = ry + ly;
129	        const wi = st.idx(tx, ty);
130	        const li = ly * rw + lx;
131	        // 天空光（不再 continue：露天格子也要继续检查火把/自发光种子，
132	        // 否则夜晚露天火把被跳过、只有背景墙后的火把才亮）
133	        const sfs = this.skyFirstSolid[tx];
134	        if (sfs === -1 || ty < sfs) {
135	          r[li] = Math.max(r[li], skyR);
136	          g[li] = Math.max(g[li], skyG);
137	          b[li] = Math.max(b[li], skyB);
138	          push(li);
139	        }
140	        // 手持光源（火把）：跟随玩家
141	        if (this.heldLight && this.heldLight.tx === tx && this.heldLight.ty === ty) {
142	          r[li] = Math.max(r[li], 255);
143	          g[li] = Math.max(g[li], 185);
144	          b[li] = Math.max(b[li], 110);
145	          push(li);
146	        }
147	        // 岩浆光源（橙红）
148	        if (st.liquid[wi] > 80 && st.liquidType[wi] === 2) {
149	          r[li] = Math.max(r[li], 255);
150	          g[li] = Math.max(g[li], 110);
151	          b[li] = Math.max(b[li], 40);
152	          push(li);
153	        }
154	        // 自发光源(可接线灯器件按开关帧判定:接线关灯后不再发光)
155	        const type = st.type[wi];
156	        if (type !== 0) {
157	          const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
158	          if (d && d.light) {
159	            const on = !d.vanilla || lightIsOn(d.vanilla.sheet, st.frameX[wi], st.frameY[wi]);
160	            if (on) {
161	              const [lr, lg, lb] = d.light;
162	              r[li] = Math.max(r[li], Math.round(lr));
163	              g[li] = Math.max(g[li], Math.round(lg));
164	              b[li] = Math.max(b[li], Math.round(lb));
165	              push(li);
166	            }
167	          }
168	        }
169	        // 液体微光（水下的折射感由 cost 体现，这里不加源）
170	      }
171	    }
172	
173	    // BFS 松弛（三通道同步）
174	    while (qh < qt) {
175	      const li = queue[qh++];
176	      const lx = li % rw, ly = (li / rw) | 0;
177	      const cvr = r[li], cvg = g[li], cvb = b[li];
178	      const maxC = Math.max(cvr, cvg, cvb);
179	      if (maxC <= 8) continue;
180	      const tx = rx + lx, ty = ry + ly;
181	      for (let dir = 0; dir < 4; dir++) {
182	        const nx = lx + (dir === 1 ? 1 : dir === 3 ? -1 : 0);
183	        const ny = ly + (dir === 0 ? -1 : dir === 2 ? 1 : 0);
184	        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= rw || ny >= rh) continue;
185	        const nli = ny * rw + nx;
186	        // 成本由目标格的阻挡度决定
187	        const wti = st.idx(rx + nx, ry + ny);
188	        const ntype = st.type[wti];
189	        let cost = LIGHT_AIR_COST;
190	        if (ntype !== 0) {
191	          const d = TILE_DEFS[ntype];
192	          if (d) cost = LIGHT_AIR_COST + Math.round(d.opacity * 30);
193	        } else if (st.liquid[wti] > 30) {
194	          cost = 10;
195	        }
196	        let changed = false;
197	        const nr = cvr - cost;
198	        if (nr > r[nli]) { r[nli] = nr; changed = true; }
199	        const ng = cvg - cost;
200	        if (ng > g[nli]) { g[nli] = ng; changed = true; }
201	        const nb = cvb - cost;
202	        if (nb > b[nli]) { b[nli] = nb; changed = true; }
203	        if (changed && qt < queue.length) queue[qt++] = nli;
204	      }
205	    }
206	  }
207	}
208	

```

```
2	// 与 LiquidRenderer 的 P3 瀑布拖尾（10 格内液体本体渐隐）互补——这里画的是
3	// 独立的 Waterfall_N 贴图柱（最长 100 格），触发条件 = 原版 halfBrick 唇缘（L100-130）。
4	// 照抄原版：
5	//  - 贴图 Waterfall_N.png：512×40 胶片条，帧宽 32、16 帧；流柱格取 (frameX,0,16,16-liquid/16)

8	//  - 走向决策（L421-507）：唇缘半砖格不满足直落门（!halfBrick）→ 先向空侧平移 1 格再落；
9	//    偏折计数 num23 仅方向反转时累加、直落清零、≥2 翻转方向而非停止
10	//  - 断流：完整实心块（blockType==0）停（L427）；溶入液池 liquid>0 && !halfBrick 停（L777）
11	//  - 撞地格绘制上移 8px：SolidTile(下) && !halfBrick（L531-534）
12	// 省略（周边系统缺失）：雨/雪云柱、彩虹/荧光砖改写、溅落 2px 斜切片、环境音、Grate 穿透。
13	import type { SpriteAtlas } from '../assets/SpriteAtlas';
14	import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
15	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
16	
17	interface Waterfall { x: number; y: number; type: number; } // type: 0 水 / 1 岩浆 / 2 蜂蜜（本仓库编码）
18	
19	const MAX_FALLS = 1000;      // 原版 qualityMax = maxWaterfallCount(1000) * gfxQuality(1)
20	const WATERFALL_DIST = 100;  // 原版 waterfallDist = 75*gfxQuality + 25
21	
22	const SHEET = ['vanilla/Waterfall_0.png', 'vanilla/Waterfall_1.png', 'vanilla/Waterfall_14.png'];
23	
24	export class WaterfallRenderer {
25	  private falls: Waterfall[] = [];
26	  private lastFind = -1;
27	  private findFrame = 0;
28	
29	  /** 扫描触发（WaterfallManager.cs FindWaterfalls 90-168，每 30 帧一次）。view 为可见 tile 窗口。
30	   *  原版条件：halfBrick 唇缘格 + 上方近干/实心 + 一侧液量>160 且对侧空 */
31	  findWaterfalls(st: TileStore, tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number, frame: number) {
32	    if (frame === this.lastFind) return;
33	    this.lastFind = frame;
34	    this.findFrame++;
35	    if (this.findFrame % 30 !== 1) return; // 与原版同节流：约 0.5s 一扫
36	    this.falls.length = 0;
37	    // 扫描窗口：原版 FindWaterfalls 屏幕四周外扩 waterfallDist(100)/下 +20（L74-81），
38	    // 视口外起点的长瀑也要登记（水柱会流进画面）
39	    const x0 = Math.max(2, tx0 - WATERFALL_DIST), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 3, tx1 + WATERFALL_DIST);
40	    const y0 = Math.max(2, ty0 - WATERFALL_DIST), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 3, ty1 + 20);
41	    const solid = (x: number, y: number) => {
42	      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
43	      const t = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];
44	      if (t === 0) return false;
45	      const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
46	      return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform;
47	    };
48	    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
49	      for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
50	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
51	        if (!st.flags[i] || !st.half[i]) continue;          // active && halfBrick（L102）
52	        const ui = i - st.w;
53	        const uq = y > 0 ? st.liquid[ui] : 0;
54	        if (!(uq < 16 || solid(x, y - 1))) continue;          // 上方近干或实心（L110）
55	        const li = i - 1, ri = i + 1;
56	        const lq = st.liquid[li], rq = st.liquid[ri];
57	        const lOpen = lq === 0 && !solid(x - 1, y) && st.slope[li] === 0;
58	        const rOpen = rq === 0 && !solid(x + 1, y) && st.slope[ri] === 0;
59	        if (!((lq > 160 || rq > 160) && (lOpen || rOpen))) continue; // L124
60	        // 类型：上/右/左三格任一岩浆→1 蜂蜜→14，否则水（L126-127）
61	        let type = 0;
62	        const isLava = (ii: number) => st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 2;
63	        const isHoney = (ii: number) => st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 3;
64	        if (isLava(ui) || isLava(ri) || isLava(li)) type = 1;
65	        else if (isHoney(ui) || isHoney(ri) || isHoney(li)) type = 2;
66	        this.falls.push({ x, y, type });
67	        if (this.falls.length >= MAX_FALLS) return;
68	      }
69	    }
70	  }
71	
72	  /** 绘制（DrawWaterfall 行 211-803 的主体路径；style 固定 0=水） */
73	  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas | null, st: TileStore, groundLevel: number, nowMs: number) {
74	    if (!atlas || this.falls.length === 0) return;
75	    const tex = SHEET.map((s) => atlas.vimages.get(s) ?? null);
76	    if (!tex[0] && !tex[1] && !tex[2]) return;
77	    // 帧动画：水 3 tick/帧、岩浆蜂蜜 7 tick/帧（1 tick ≈ 16.67ms）
78	    const regular = Math.floor(nowMs / 50) % 16;
79	    const slow = Math.floor(nowMs / 117) % 16;
80	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
81	    for (const wf of this.falls) {
82	      const texImg = tex[wf.type] ?? tex[0];
83	      if (!texImg) continue;
84	      const frameX = 32 * (wf.type === 0 ? regular : slow);
85	      let cx = wf.x, cy = wf.y; // 原版从唇缘格本体开始画（半砖 blockType=1 不断流，L427）
86	      let dx = 0, dy = 0;       // num26/num27：本步位移
87	      let lastH = 0;            // num14：上一次水平方向
88	      let turns = 0;            // num23：方向反转计数
89	      // SolidTile 语义（WorldGen.SolidTile：实心 && !platform && !half && !slope）
90	      const solidT = (x: number, y: number) => {
91	        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
92	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
93	        const t = st.type[i];
94	        if (t === 0) return false;
95	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
96	        return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
97	      };
98	      for (let step = 0; step < WATERFALL_DIST; step++) {
99	        if (cx < 1 || cy < 1 || cx >= st.w - 1 || cy >= st.h - 1) break;
100	        const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
101	        // 完整实心块（blockType==0）断流；半砖/坡面豁免（L427）
102	        if (solidT(cx, cy) && !st.half[ci] && st.slope[ci] === 0) break;
103	        const li = ci - 1, ri = ci + 1, bi = ci + st.w;
104	        // ---- 走向决策（L452-507，坡面分支省略）----
105	        if ((!solidT(cx, cy + 1) || st.type[bi] === 0) && !st.half[ci]) {
106	          // 直落门（L470）：下方非实心 且 本格非半砖——唇缘半砖格不直落
107	          turns = 0; dy = 1; dx = 0;
108	        } else if ((solidT(cx - 1, cy) || st.liquid[li] > 0) && (!solidT(cx + 1, cy) && st.liquid[ri] === 0)) {
109	          // 左堵/左液 → 向右平移（L476-483）
110	          if (lastH === -1) turns++;
111	          dx = 1; dy = 0; lastH = 1;
112	        } else if ((solidT(cx + 1, cy) || st.liquid[ri] > 0) && (!solidT(cx - 1, cy) && st.liquid[li] === 0)) {
113	          // 右堵/右液 → 向左平移（L484-491）
114	          if (lastH === 1) turns++;
115	          dx = -1; dy = 0; lastH = -1;
116	        } else if (!solidT(cx + 1, cy) && st.liquid[ri] === 0 && !solidT(cx - 1, cy) && st.liquid[li] === 0) {
117	          dy = 0; dx = lastH;   // 两侧皆空：保持上次水平方向（L492-496）
118	        } else {
119	          turns++; dy = 0; dx = 0; // 四面皆堵：滞留计数（L497-501）
120	        }
121	        if (turns >= 2) { lastH *= -1; dx *= -1; } // 翻转方向而非停止（L503-507）
122	        // ---- 绘制本格 ----
123	        const liq = st.liquid[ci];
124	        let alpha = wf.type === 1 ? 1.0 : wf.type === 2 ? 0.8
125	          : (st.wall[ci] !== 0 || cy >= groundLevel ? 0.6 : 1.0);
126	        if (step > WATERFALL_DIST - 10) alpha *= (WATERFALL_DIST - step) / 10;
127	        const sh = 16 - Math.floor(liq / 16);       // num21：按格内液量裁掉底部
128	        const yOff = solidT(cx, cy + 1) && !st.half[ci] ? 8 : 0; // 撞地格上移 8px（L531-534）
129	        if (sh > 0) {
130	          ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
131	          ctx.drawImage(texImg, frameX, 0, 16, sh, cx * 16, cy * 16 + yOff, 16, sh);
132	        }
133	        if (liq > 0 && !st.half[ci]) break;         // 溶入液池（L777-778）
134	        cx += dx; cy += dy;
135	      }
136	    }
137	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
138	  }
139	
140	  get count() { return this.falls.length; }
141	}
142
```

```
1	// 原版背景墙渲染器：TEdit WallFraming.cs 精确移植（= vanilla Framing.WallFrame 1.4.5.4）。

5	import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';

15	  /* 4 E    */ [[9, 0], [9, 1], [9, 2], [9, 5]],

17	  /* 6 W+E  */ [[6, 4], [7, 4], [8, 4], [5, 6]],
18	  /* 7 N+W+E */ [[1, 2], [2, 2], [3, 2], [3, 5]],
19	  /* 8 S    */ [[6, 0], [7, 0], [8, 0], [6, 5]],
20	  /* 9 N+S  */ [[5, 0], [5, 1], [5, 2], [5, 5]],
21	  /* 10 W+S */ [[1, 3], [3, 3], [5, 3], [1, 6]],
22	  /* 11 N+W+S */ [[4, 0], [4, 1], [4, 2], [4, 5]],
23	  /* 12 E+S */ [[0, 3], [2, 3], [4, 3], [0, 6]],
24	  /* 13 N+E+S */ [[0, 0], [0, 1], [0, 2], [0, 5]],
25	  /* 14 W+E+S */ [[1, 0], [2, 0], [3, 0], [1, 5]],
26	  /* 15 ALL center0 */ [[1, 1], [2, 1], [3, 1], [2, 5]],
27	  /* 16 ALL center1 */ [[6, 1], [7, 1], [8, 1], [7, 5]],
28	  /* 17 ALL center2 */ [[6, 2], [7, 2], [8, 2], [8, 5]],
29	  /* 18 ALL center3 */ [[10, 0], [10, 1], [10, 2], [10, 5]],
30	  /* 19 ALL center4 */ [[11, 0], [11, 1], [11, 2], [11, 5]],
31	];
32	
33	// 全邻时的中心子模式：CenterWallFrameLookup[y%3][x%3]（TEdit 源序）
34	const CENTER = [
35	  [2, 0, 0],
36	  [0, 1, 4],
37	  [0, 3, 0],
38	];
39	
40	// Phlebas（largeFrame 1）：4×3 重复块，1-based
41	const PHLEBAS = [
42	  [2, 4, 2],
43	  [1, 3, 1],
44	  [2, 2, 4],
45	  [1, 1, 3],
46	];
47	
48	// Lazure（largeFrame 2）：2×2 重复块，1-based
49	const LAZURE = [
50	  [1, 3],
51	  [2, 4],
52	];
53	
54	const STRIDE = 36; // 32px 帧 + 4px 间隔
55	
56	export class VanillaWallTiler {
57	  private imgCache = new Map<number, HTMLImageElement | null>();
58	
59	  constructor(private atlas: SpriteAtlas | null) {}
60	
61	  private wallImg(wallId: number): HTMLImageElement | null {
62	    let img = this.imgCache.get(wallId);
63	    if (img === undefined) {
64	      const m = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vanilla.walls[String(wallId)] : null;
65	      img = m && this.atlas ? this.atlas.vimages.get(m.sheet) ?? null : null;
66	      this.imgCache.set(wallId, img);
67	    }
68	    return img;
69	  }
70	
71	  /** 邻居判定（TEdit HasWallNeighbor）：wall>0 任意 id 都算，或截断墙方块。
72	   *  truncates = 内部 tile id 数组（wld 54 玻璃 / 328 / 459 / 748 对应的 v_ 键） */
73	  private hasNeighbor(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, truncates: number[]): boolean {
74	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
75	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
76	    if (st.wall[i] > 0) return true;
77	    if (st.flags[i] && truncates.includes(st.type[i])) return true;
78	    return false;
79	  }
80	
81	  /** 绘制一格墙（源 32×32，目标以格为中心的 2×2 格大小） */
82	  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, wallId: number, truncates: number[], px: number, py: number): void {
83	    const img = this.wallImg(wallId);
84	    if (!img) return;
85	    // 掩码：N=1 W=2 E=4 S=8
86	    let mask = 0;
87	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y - 1, truncates)) mask |= 1;
88	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x - 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 2;
89	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x + 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 4;
90	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y + 1, truncates)) mask |= 8;
91	    // 全邻 → 中心子模式
92	    if (mask === 15) mask += CENTER[y % 3][x % 3];
93	    // 变体（largeFrame 三模式）
94	    const lf = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vanilla.walls[String(wallId)]?.largeFrame ?? 0 : 0;
95	    let v: number;
96	    if (lf === 1) v = PHLEBAS[y % 4][x % 3] - 1;
97	    else if (lf === 2) v = LAZURE[x % 2][y % 2] - 1;
98	    else v = ((x * 7) + (y * 11)) % 3;
99	    const entry = WALL_LOOKUP[mask];
100	    if (!entry) return;
101	    const fr = entry[v] ?? entry[0];
102	    const sx = fr[0] * STRIDE, sy = fr[1] * STRIDE;
103	    if (sx + 32 > img.width || sy + 32 > img.height) return; // 变体3 超出矮表（默认模式不会发生）
104	    ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, 32, 32, px - TILE / 2, py - TILE / 2, 32, 32);
105	  }
106	
107	  /** 墙帧（col,row）——调试/纠错面板定位用 */
108	  frameAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, wallId: number, truncates: number[]): { col: number; row: number } {
109	    let mask = 0;
110	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y - 1, truncates)) mask |= 1;
111	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x - 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 2;
112	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x + 1, y, truncates)) mask |= 4;
113	    if (this.hasNeighbor(st, x, y + 1, truncates)) mask |= 8;
114	    if (mask === 15) mask += CENTER[y % 3][x % 3];
115	    const lf = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vanilla.walls[String(wallId)]?.largeFrame ?? 0 : 0;
116	    let v: number;
117	    if (lf === 1) v = PHLEBAS[y % 4][x % 3] - 1;
118	    else if (lf === 2) v = LAZURE[x % 2][y % 2] - 1;
119	    else v = ((x * 7) + (y * 11)) % 3;
120	    const fr = WALL_LOOKUP[mask]?.[v] ?? [1, 1];
121	    return { col: fr[0], row: fr[1] };
122	  }
123	
124	  hasTexture(wallId: number): boolean {
125	    return this.wallImg(wallId) !== null;
126	  }
127	}
128	

```

```
190	  private update(e: LiquidEntry) {

271	  private sideFlow(x: number, y: number, i5: number) {

348	        st.liquidType[il] = myType;
349	        if (lq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); }
350	        st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
351	        if (rq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
352	        st.liquidType[ip2] = myType;
353	        if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); }

358	        if (m === 254 && Math.random() * 30 < 1) m = 255;
359	        if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else st.liquidType[il] = myType;
360	        if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
361	        st.liquid[i5] = m;

367	      st.liquidType[il] = myType;

380	  // ================= 液体交互（Liquid.cs:888-1115） =================
381	
382	  /** 岩浆格与水/蜂蜜接触 → 黑曜石 56 / 脆蜂蜜块 230（阈值 24） */
383	  private lavaCheck(x: number, y: number) {
384	    const st = this.world.store;
385	    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:898）
386	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
387	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;
388	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];
389	    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];
390	    if ((lq > 0 && lt !== 2) || (rq > 0 && rt !== 2) || (uq > 0 && ut !== 2)) {
391	      // 情形 A：左右/上方有非岩浆液体 → 生成在岩浆格（Liquid.cs:905-959）
392	      let sum = 0;
393	      let type = OBSIDIAN;
394	      if (lt !== 2) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }
395	      if (rt !== 2) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }
396	      if (ut !== 2) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }
397	      if (lt === 3 || rt === 3 || ut === 3) type = CRISPY_HONEY;
398	      if (sum < 24) return;
399	      const t = st.type[i5];
400	      if (t !== 0) {
401	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
402	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);  // 近似 tileObsidianKill
403	        else return;                             // 平台等保留方块 → 不生成
404	      }
405	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
406	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
407	      st.setTile(x, y, type);
408	    } else {
409	      if (dq <= 0 || dt === 2) return;
410	      // 情形 B：仅下方有水/蜂蜜 → 生成在下方格（Liquid.cs:961-1014）
411	      const belowT = st.type[id];
412	      if (belowT !== 0) {
413	        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];
414	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0); // tileCut / obsidianKill 近似
415	        else return;
416	      }
417	      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }
418	      const type = dt === 3 ? CRISPY_HONEY : OBSIDIAN;
419	      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
420	      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;
421	      st.setTile(x, y + 1, type);
422	    }
423	  }
424	
425	  /** 蜂蜜格与水接触 → 蜂蜜块 229（阈值 32） */
426	  private honeyCheck(x: number, y: number) {
427	    const st = this.world.store;
428	    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:1020）
429	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
430	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;
431	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];
432	    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];
433	    if ((lq > 0 && lt === 1) || (rq > 0 && rt === 1) || (uq > 0 && ut === 1)) {
434	      let sum = 0;
435	      if (lt === 1) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }
436	      if (rt === 1) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }
437	      if (ut === 1) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }
438	      if (sum < 32) return;
439	      const t = st.type[i5];
440	      if (t !== 0) {
441	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
442	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);
443	        else return;
444	      }
445	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
446	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
447	      st.setTile(x, y, HONEY_BLOCK);
448	    } else {
449	      if (dq <= 0 || dt !== 1) return;
450	      const belowT = st.type[id];
451	      if (belowT !== 0) {
452	        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];
453	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0);
454	        else return;
455	      }
456	      if (st.liquid[i5] < 32) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }
457	      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
458	      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;
459	      st.setTile(x, y + 1, HONEY_BLOCK);
460	    }
461	  }

463	  // ================= DelWater（Liquid.cs:1117-1213） =================
464	
465	  private delWater(l: number) {
466	    const st = this.world.store;
467	    const e = this.liquids[l];
468	    const x = e.x, y = e.y;
469	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
470	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, id = i5 + st.w;
471	    const amt = st.liquid[i5];
472	    if (amt < 2) {
473	      // 残量清零 + 邻居唤醒（Liquid.cs:1125-1137）
474	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
475	      if (st.liquid[il] < 2) st.liquid[il] = 0; else this.addWater(x - 1, y);
476	      if (st.liquid[ir] < 2) st.liquid[ir] = 0; else this.addWater(x + 1, y);
477	    } else if (amt < 20) {
478	      // 水膜抹除：邻格更低或下方不满（Liquid.cs:1138-1142）
479	      const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], dq = st.liquid[id];
480	      if ((lq < amt && !this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y)) ||
481	          (rq < amt && !this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y)) ||
482	          (dq < 255 && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1))) {
483	        st.liquid[i5] = 0;
484	      }
485	    } else if (st.liquid[id] < 255 && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1) && !this.stuck && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y)) {
486	      e.kill = 0; // 下方未满：留在列表继续流（Liquid.cs:1143-1147）
487	      return;
488	    }
489	    if (st.liquid[i5] < 250 && st.liquid[i5 - st.w] > 0) this.addWater(x, y - 1);
490	    if (st.liquid[i5] === 0) {
491	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
492	    } else {
493	      const rq = st.liquid[ir], lq = st.liquid[il];
494	      if (rq > 0 && rq < 250 && !this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y) && st.liquid[i5] !== rq) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
495	      if (lq > 0 && lq < 250 && !this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y) && st.liquid[i5] !== lq) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
496	      if (st.liquidType[i5] === 2) {
497	        this.lavaCheck(x, y);
498	        // 3×3 草转泥土近似：岩浆旁 decor 植物清除（Liquid.cs:1160-1187 原版为草方块转换）
499	        for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
500	          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
501	            const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
502	            if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nx >= st.w - 1 || ny >= st.h - 1) continue;
503	            const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
504	            const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[ni]];
505	            if (d && d.decor && d.attach === 'ground') st.setTile(nx, ny, 0);
506	          }
507	        }
508	      } else if (st.liquidType[i5] === 3) {
509	        this.honeyCheck(x, y);
510	      }
511	    }
512	    // swap-remove（Liquid.cs:1193-1197）：只搬 x/y/kill，delay 保留本槽旧值（原版语义）
513	    this.numLiquid--;
514	    this.checking[i5] = 0;
515	    const tail = this.liquids[this.numLiquid];
516	    e.x = tail.x; e.y = tail.y; e.kill = tail.kill;
517	    this.liquids.length = this.numLiquid;
518	  }
519	
520	  // ================= 读档沉降：QuickWater（Liquid.cs:85-103 / 105-212） =================
521	
522	  /** 自底向上逐湿格直接搬运沉降（原版 QuickWater(verbose, -1, -1)：y 从 h-3 到 3） */
523	  quickWater(minY = 3, maxY = -1) {
524	    const st = this.world.store;
525	    const yMax = maxY < 0 ? st.h - 3 : maxY;
526	    for (let y = yMax; y >= minY; y--) {
527	      for (let x = 4; x < st.w - 4; x++) {
528	        if (st.liquid[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0) this.settleWaterAt(x, y);
529	      }
530	    }
531	  }
532	
533	  /** 单格液体直接搬到最终落点（Liquid.cs:105-212 逐行对照） */
534	  private settleWaterAt(originX: number, originY: number) {
535	    const st = this.world.store;
536	    const oi = this.idx(originX, originY);
537	    if (st.liquid[oi] === 0) return;
538	    let X = originX, Y = originY;
539	    const srcType = st.liquidType[oi];
540	    let liquid = st.liquid[oi];
541	    st.liquid[oi] = 0;
542	    let flag1 = true;
543	    for (;;) {
544	      // 1) 垂直下落：下方空且可通行就一直落（Liquid.cs:121-130）
545	      let flag2 = false;
546	      while (Y < st.h - 5 && st.liquid[this.idx(X, Y + 1)] === 0 && !this.blocksLiquid(X, Y + 1)) {
547	        Y++;
548	        flag2 = true;
549	        flag1 = false;
550	      }
551	      // （Liquid.cs:129-130 的 waterLine 岩浆转换仅世界生成期生效，读档跳过）
552	      // 2) 蛇形横向铺开（Liquid.cs:131-195）
553	      let dir = -1;          // num2：当前行走方向
554	      let step = 0;          // num3：当前行步数
555	      let lastDir = -1;      // num4：最后空位方向
556	      let lastStep = 0;      // num5：最后空位步数
557	      let hitL = false;      // flag4：左边界
558	      let hitR = false;      // flag3：右边界
559	      let dropped = false;   // flag5：本轮发生下落
560	      for (;;) {
561	        const probeX = X + step * dir;
562	        // 越界防护：原版 C# 越界会抛异常，JS 的 idx 会静默回绕到上一行——
563	        // 超出 [0, w-1] 的探测一律视为边界命中，不再读取
564	        if (probeX < 0 || probeX >= st.w) {
565	          if (dir === -1) hitL = true; else hitR = true;
566	          if (hitL && hitR) break;
567	          if (hitR) { dir = -1; step++; }
568	          else if (hitL) { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = 1; }
569	          else { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = -dir; }
570	          continue;
571	        }
572	        const ci = this.idx(probeX, Y);
573	        if (st.liquid[ci] === 0) { lastDir = dir; lastStep = step; }
574	        if (dir === -1 && probeX < 5) hitL = true;
575	        else if (dir === 1 && probeX > st.w - 5) hitR = true;
576	        const bi = this.idx(probeX, Y + 1);
577	        // 侧下方同类型未满 → 直接灌入（Liquid.cs:149-159）
578	        if (st.liquid[bi] !== 0 && st.liquid[bi] !== 255 && st.liquidType[bi] === srcType) {
579	          let t = 255 - st.liquid[bi];
580	          if (t > liquid) t = liquid;
581	          st.liquid[bi] += t;
582	          liquid -= t;
583	          if (liquid === 0) break;
584	        }
585	        if (Y >= st.h - 5 || st.liquid[bi] !== 0 || this.blocksLiquid(probeX, Y + 1)) {
586	          // 下方堵住 → 沿行继续 / 折返（Liquid.cs:162-191）
587	          const aheadX = X + (step + 1) * dir;
588	          const inRange = aheadX >= 0 && aheadX < st.w;
589	          const ni = inRange ? this.idx(aheadX, Y) : -1;
590	          const nAmt = ni >= 0 ? st.liquid[ni] : 0;
591	          const nSolid = inRange ? this.blocksLiquid(aheadX, Y) : true;
592	          if ((nAmt !== 0 && (!flag1 || dir !== 1)) || nSolid) {
593	            if (dir === 1) hitR = true; else hitL = true;
594	          }
595	          if (hitL && hitR) break;
596	          if (hitR) { dir = -1; step++; }
597	          else if (hitL) { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = 1; }
598	          else { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = -dir; }
599	        } else {
600	          dropped = true;
601	          break;
602	        }
603	      }
604	      // 落点写回钳制到有效列（防 lastDir/lastStep 记录异常时把液体写出世界）
605	      X = Math.max(2, Math.min(st.w - 3, X + lastStep * lastDir));
606	      if (liquid !== 0 && dropped) Y++;
607	      else break;
608	    }
609	    // 3) 写回落点（Liquid.cs:204-205）
610	    const fi = this.idx(X, Y);
611	    st.liquid[fi] = liquid;
612	    st.liquidType[fi] = srcType;
613	    if (st.liquid[fi] > 0) {
614	      // 落地即交互（Liquid.cs:206-210 / AttemptToMoveLava/Honey 248-280）
615	      this.attemptToMoveLiquid(X, Y, srcType, 2);
616	      this.attemptToMoveLiquid(X, Y, srcType, 3);
617	    }
618	  }
619	
620	  /** 落点与异种液体相邻时触发交互检查（Liquid.cs:248-280 AttemptToMoveLava 同构）。
621	   *  原版语义：邻居的 target 性 != 落点格的 target 性 才算"异种相遇"——
622	   *  水落水旁不算，水落岩浆旁才算（否则整片海洋互相当岩浆转黑曜石） */
623	  private attemptToMoveLiquid(X: number, Y: number, srcType: number, targetType: number) {
624	    const st = this.world.store;
625	    const srcIs = srcType === targetType;
626	    const check = targetType === 2 ? (x: number, y: number) => this.lavaCheck(x, y)
627	      : (x: number, y: number) => this.honeyCheck(x, y);
628	    const diff = (x: number, y: number) => {
629	      const i = this.idx(x, y);
630	      return st.liquid[i] > 0 && (st.liquidType[i] === targetType) !== srcIs;
631	    };
632	    if (diff(X - 1, Y)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X - 1, Y); }
633	    else if (diff(X + 1, Y)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X + 1, Y); }
634	    else if (diff(X, Y - 1)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X, Y - 1); }
635	    else if (diff(X, Y + 1)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X, Y + 1); }
636	  }
637	
638	  // ================= WaterCheck（WorldGen.cs:48955-49009） =================
639	
640	  /** 全图自底向上重建活动列表：能流的/液面不齐的/岩浆旁有异种的都入列 */
641	  waterCheck() {
642	    const st = this.world.store;
643	    this.liquids.length = 0;
644	    this.numLiquid = 0;
645	    this.buffer.length = 0; // 原版 WaterCheck 首行清 LiquidBuffer（WorldGen.cs:48959）
646	    this.checking.fill(0);
647	    for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
648	      for (let y = st.h - 2; y > 0; y--) {
649	        const i = this.idx(x, y);
650	        if (st.liquid[i] === 0) continue;
651	        const t = st.type[i];
652	        if (t !== 0) {
653	          const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
654	          if (d && d.solid && !d.platform) { st.liquid[i] = 0; continue; } // 实心格内液体清零
655	        }
656	        const id = i + st.w;
657	        const dq = st.liquid[id];
658	        if (!this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1) && dq < 255) {
659	          if (dq > 250) st.liquid[id] = 255;
660	          else this.addWater(x, y);
661	        }
662	        const il = i - 1, ir = i + 1;
663	        if (!this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y) && st.liquid[il] !== st.liquid[i]) this.addWater(x, y);
664	        else if (!this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y) && st.liquid[ir] !== st.liquid[i]) this.addWater(x, y);
665	        if (st.liquidType[i] === 2) {
666	          const iu = i - st.w;
667	          if ((st.liquid[il] > 0 && st.liquidType[il] !== 2) ||
668	              (st.liquid[ir] > 0 && st.liquidType[ir] !== 2) ||
669	              (st.liquid[iu] > 0 && st.liquidType[iu] !== 2) ||
670	              (dq > 0 && st.liquidType[id] !== 2)) this.addWater(x, y);
671	        }
672	      }
673	    }
674	  }
675	}
676
```

```
26	    let c = this.rotCache.get(key);
27	    if (!c) {
28	      c = document.createElement('canvas');
29	      c.width = base.sw; c.height = base.sh;
30	      const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
31	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
32	      ctx.translate(base.sw / 2, base.sh / 2);
33	      // 位置旋转 (x,y)→(-y,x)（Unity y-up 的 90° 逆时针视觉方向）
34	      // 在 y-down 画布上等价的视觉旋转是顺时针 → rotate 负号
35	      ctx.rotate((-rot * Math.PI) / 2);
36	      ctx.drawImage(base.img, base.sx, base.sy, base.sw, base.sh, -base.sw / 2, -base.sh / 2, base.sw, base.sh);
37	      this.rotCache.set(key, c);
38	    }
39	    return { img: c, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: c.width, sh: c.height };
40	  }
41	
42	  /** 对 (x,y) 求贴合贴图。layer='tile' 比较 tile 类型；'wall' 比较墙类型 */
43	  tile(
44	    ruleName: string, store: TileStore,
45	    x: number, y: number, tileType: number,
46	    layer: 'tile' | 'wall' = 'tile',
47	  ): DrawRect | null {
48	    // 人工标注驱动（annotator.html 导出），如 '@grass'
49	    if (ruleName.startsWith('@')) {
50	      return this.annotatedTile(ruleName.slice(1), store, x, y, tileType);
51	    }
52	    const def: RuleTileDef | undefined = this.atlas.resources.rules[ruleName];
53	    if (!def) return null;
54	    // 泥土/石头：四邻无空气暴露（全被实心或草皮包围）→ 无缝内格精灵，
55	    // 消除地形内部出现带缝隙的边缘变体；仅真正临空才用边缘件
56	    if (ruleName === 'Dirt' || ruleName === 'Stone') {
57	      let exposed = false;
58	      for (const [px, py] of [[0, -1], [1, 0], [0, 1], [-1, 0]]) {
59	        if (!store.inBounds(x + px, y + py)) continue;
60	        if (store.type[store.idx(x + px, y + py)] === 0) { exposed = true; break; }
61	      }
62	      if (!exposed) {
63	        const base = this.baseSprite(ruleName, x, y);
64	        if (base) return base;
65	      }
66	    }
67	    const isSame = (nx: number, ny: number): 0 | 1 | 2 => {
68	      // 0=air 1=this 2=other
69	      if (!store.inBounds(nx, ny)) return 0;
70	      const i = store.idx(nx, ny);
71	      const v = layer === 'wall' ? store.wall[i] : store.type[i];
72	      if (v === 0) return 0;
73	      return v === tileType ? 1 : 2;
74	    };
75	    // 草皮禁用规则旋转：草面必须始终朝上（侧面暴露在原版是草皮垂落，
76	    // 旋转精灵会把草面转到侧面 —— Maples 规则集的角落规则会在错误旋转下命中）
77	    const noRotate = ruleName === 'Grass';
78	    for (const rule of def.tilingRules) {
79	      const rotations = !noRotate && rule.transform === 1 ? [0, 1, 2, 3] : [0];
80	      for (const rot of rotations) {
81	        let ok = true;
82	        for (let i = 0; i < rule.positions.length && i < rule.neighbors.length; i++) {
83	          let [px, py] = rule.positions[i];
84	          for (let r = 0; r < rot; r++) [px, py] = rotPos(px, py);
85	          const want = rule.neighbors[i];
86	          if (want === 0) continue; // Any
87	          // Unity 规则坐标 y 向上，本引擎 y 向下 → 取反
88	          const actual = isSame(x + px, y - py);
89	          const wantThis = want === 1 || want === 3 || want === 4;
90	          const wantNot = want === 2 || want === 5;
91	          const wantAir = want === 6;
92	          if (wantThis && actual !== 1) { ok = false; break; }
93	          if (wantNot && actual !== 2 && actual !== 0) { ok = false; break; }
94	          if (wantAir && actual !== 0) { ok = false; break; }
95	        }
96	        if (!ok || rule.sprites.length === 0) continue;
97	        // 树干误中枝干规则：枝干规则的邻居条件（一侧上中下皆树）对相邻的
98	        // 树干格同样成立 → 树干会随机画出整条枝干（浮空断干）。
99	        // 竖向连接树干的格子只从非 Branches 的树干变体池里选
100	        let pool = rule.sprites;
101	        if (ruleName === 'Tree') {
102	          const hasVert = isSame(x, y - 1) === 1 || isSame(x, y + 1) === 1;
103	          if (hasVert) {
104	            const noBranch = rule.sprites.filter((s) => !s.sprite.includes('Branches'));
105	            if (noBranch.length) pool = noBranch;
106	          }
107	        }
108	        // m_Output 1 = Random：按坐标 hash 选变体
109	        const idx = rule.output === 1 && pool.length > 1
110	          ? Math.floor(hash2(x, y, 71) * pool.length) % pool.length
111	          : 0;
112	        const sp = pool[Math.min(idx, pool.length - 1)];
113	        const base = this.atlas.rect(sp.file, sp.sprite);
114	        if (!base) continue;
115	        return this.rotatedRect(sp, base, rot);
116	      }
117	    }
118	    if (def.defaultSprite) {
119	      // 草皮兜底：默认精灵是"左侧垂落"侧向贴图，直接用会把侧面/底部暴露画成左旋。
120	      // 改为按暴露方向从绿边分类组里选。
121	      if (ruleName === 'Grass') {
122	        const fb = this.grassFallback(store, x, y, tileType);
123	        if (fb) return fb;
124	      }
125	      return this.atlas.rect(def.defaultSprite.file, def.defaultSprite.sprite);
126	    }
127	    return null;
128	  }
129	
130	  /** 树苗精灵：Tree_Bodys 首个树干段（16×20 的树苗样子） */
131	  saplingSprite(x: number, y: number): DrawRect | null {
132	    const r = this.atlas.rect('地形/Tree_Bodys.png', 'Tree_Bodys_0');
133	    return r ?? this.weedSprite(x, y);
134	  }
135	
136	  /** 杂草精灵（Tiles_3，16×20，按坐标 hash 选变体） */
137	  private weedCache: DrawRect[] | null = null;
138	  weedSprite(x: number, y: number): DrawRect | null {
139	    if (!this.weedCache) {
140	      const list: DrawRect[] = [];
141	      for (let k = 0; k <= 14; k++) {
142	        const r = this.atlas.rect('地形/Tiles_3.png', 'Tiles_3_' + k);
143	        if (r) list.push(r);
144	      }
145	      this.weedCache = list;
146	    }
147	    if (!this.weedCache.length) return null;
148	    return this.weedCache[Math.floor(hash2(x, y, 401) * this.weedCache.length) % this.weedCache.length];
149	  }
150	
151	  /**
152	   * 原版架构：草皮 = 不透明泥土内格底 + 带透明缺口的草皮覆盖。
153	   * 返回指定规则集的"内格"精灵（全邻同类规则），供草皮垫底。
154	   */
155	  baseSprite(ruleName: string, x: number, y: number): DrawRect | null {
156	    const def = this.atlas.resources.rules[ruleName];
157	    if (!def) return null;
158	    // 找"全邻 This"的内格规则（neighbors 全为 1/3/4）
159	    for (const rule of def.tilingRules) {
160	      const allThis = rule.neighbors.length > 0 &&
161	        rule.neighbors.every((v) => v === 1 || v === 3 || v === 4);
162	      if (allThis && rule.sprites.length) {
163	        const sp = rule.sprites[Math.floor(hash2(x, y, 71) * rule.sprites.length) % rule.sprites.length];
164	        const r = this.atlas.rect(sp.file, sp.sprite);
165	        if (r) return r;
166	      }
167	    }
168	    if (def.defaultSprite) return this.atlas.rect(def.defaultSprite.file, def.defaultSprite.sprite);
169	    return null;
170	  }
171	
172	  // ---- 草皮数据驱动兜底：按绿边方位分类精灵，按暴露方向选择 ----
173	  private grassGroups: Record<string, Array<{ key: string; rect: DrawRect }>> | null = null;
174	
175	  private analyzeGrass() {
176	    const file = '地形/Tiles_2.png';
177	    const entry = this.atlas.data.files[file];
178	    const img = this.atlas.images.get(file);
179	    if (!entry || !img) return;
180	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
181	    c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
182	    const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
183	    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
184	    const groups: Record<string, Array<{ key: string; rect: DrawRect }>> = {};
185	    for (const s of entry.sprites) {
186	      if (s.w !== 16 || s.h !== 16) continue;
187	      const rect = this.atlas.rect(file, s.name);
188	      if (!rect) continue;
189	      const d = ctx.getImageData(rect.sx, rect.sy, 16, 16).data;
190	      const greenRatio = (x0: number, y0: number, bw: number, bh: number) => {
191	        let n = 0, t = 0;
192	        for (let y = y0; y < y0 + bh; y++) {
193	          for (let x = x0; x < x0 + bw; x++) {
194	            const i = (y * 16 + x) * 4;
195	            if (d[i + 3] < 40) continue;
196	            t++;
197	            if (d[i + 1] > d[i] + 30 && d[i + 1] > d[i + 2] + 30) n++;
198	          }
199	        }
200	        return t ? n / t : 0;
201	      };
202	      const top = greenRatio(0, 0, 16, 4);
203	      const bottom = greenRatio(0, 12, 16, 4);
204	      const left = greenRatio(0, 0, 4, 16);
205	      const right = greenRatio(12, 0, 4, 16);
206	      const max = Math.max(top, bottom, left, right);
207	      if (max < 0.55) continue; // 绿边不明显，非草皮边缘块
208	      // 只收完整方块（覆盖率高）：排除角落残片/装饰碎块
209	      let opaque = 0;
210	      for (let j = 3; j < d.length; j += 4) if (d[j] > 40) opaque++;
211	      if (opaque / 256 < 0.75) continue;
212	      // 取最强侧；不显著高于对侧（斜角/全绿件）一律归 T，避免杂件混入侧组
213	      let side: string;
214	      if (max === bottom && bottom > top * 1.5) side = 'B';
215	      else if (max === left && left > right * 1.5) side = 'L';
216	      else if (max === right && right > left * 1.5) side = 'R';
217	      else side = 'T';
218	      if (!groups[side]) groups[side] = [];
219	      groups[side].push({ key: s.name, rect });
220	    }
221	    this.grassGroups = groups;
222	  }
223	
224	  private grassFallback(store: TileStore, x: number, y: number, tileType: number): DrawRect | null {
225	    if (!this.grassGroups) this.analyzeGrass();
226	    const g = this.grassGroups!;
227	    if (!g.T && !g.L && !g.R && !g.B) return null;
228	    const air = (nx: number, ny: number) => {
229	      if (!store.inBounds(nx, ny)) return true;
230	      return store.type[store.idx(nx, ny)] === 0;
231	    };
232	    const sideAir = [air(x, y - 1), air(x + 1, y), air(x, y + 1), air(x - 1, y)]; // 上右下左
233	    // 优先：暴露侧的垂落变体；多侧暴露取上 > 侧 > 下；无暴露用顶绿
234	    let want = 'T';
235	    if (sideAir[3] && g.L?.length) want = 'L';
236	    else if (sideAir[1] && g.R?.length) want = 'R';
237	    else if (sideAir[0] && g.T?.length) want = 'T';
238	    else if (sideAir[2] && g.B?.length) want = 'B';
239	    const list = g[want] ?? g.T;
240	    if (!list?.length) return null;
241	    const pick = list[Math.floor(hash2(x, y, 977) * list.length) % list.length];
242	    return pick.rect;
243	  }
244	
245	  // ---- 人工标注驱动（annotator.html 导出的方位标签）----
246	  private annGroups: Record<string, Record<string, DrawRect[]>> = {};
247	  // 镂空补齐缓存：sprite 名 → 合成画布（透明像素用件自身泥土色填补，
248	  // 相邻草块连续无缝，边缘又是覆盖件自身质感，不露程序化泥土底）
249	  private filledCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
250	
251	  private filledRect(r: DrawRect, key: string): DrawRect {
252	    let c = this.filledCache.get(key);
253	    if (!c) {
254	      c = document.createElement('canvas');
255	      c.width = r.sw; c.height = r.sh;
256	      const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
257	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
258	      ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, 0, 0, r.sw, r.sh);
259	      const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, r.sw, r.sh);
260	      const d = img.data;
261	      const w = r.sw, h = r.sh;
262	      // 多源 BFS：每个透明/半透明像素取最近不透明邻像素的颜色（图像修复式填补），
263	      // 草边延伸草色、土边延伸土色 —— 避免"平均色"造成的灰绿污染
264	      const dist = new Int16Array(w * h).fill(-1);
265	      const queue: number[] = [];
266	      // 源阈值 128：PNG 提取管线的 alpha 常停在 254，用 255 会找不到源 → 全黑
267	      for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
268	        for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
269	          const i = (y * w + x) * 4;
270	          if (d[i + 3] >= 128) {
271	            dist[y * w + x] = 0;
272	            queue.push(y * w + x);
273	          }
274	        }
275	      }
276	      // 兜底：完全没有源像素时（极端情况），整块用中心色填充避免黑块
277	      if (queue.length === 0) {
278	        const ci = ((h >> 1) * w + (w >> 1)) * 4;
279	        for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) {
280	          d[i] = d[ci]; d[i + 1] = d[ci + 1]; d[i + 2] = d[ci + 2]; d[i + 3] = 255;
281	        }
282	      }
283	      let qh = 0;
284	      while (qh < queue.length) {
285	        const p = queue[qh++];
286	        const px = p % w, py = (p / w) | 0;
287	        for (const [dx, dy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]]) {
288	          const nx = px + dx, ny = py + dy;
289	          if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
290	          const np = ny * w + nx;
291	          if (dist[np] !== -1) continue;
292	          dist[np] = dist[p] + 1;
293	          queue.push(np);
294	          // 继承传播源颜色（首层即原始不透明像素色），硬 alpha 255
295	          const si = p * 4, ni = np * 4;
296	          d[ni] = d[si]; d[ni + 1] = d[si + 1]; d[ni + 2] = d[si + 2]; d[ni + 3] = 255;
297	        }
298	      }
299	      ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
300	      this.filledCache.set(key, c);
301	    }
302	    return { img: c, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: c.width, sh: c.height };
303	  }
304	
305	  private annotatedTile(annKey: string, store: TileStore, x: number, y: number, tileType: number): DrawRect | null {
306	    // annKey: 'grass' → 表 '地形/Tiles_2.png'；其它表可在此扩展
307	    const sheetByAnn: Record<string, string> = { grass: '地形/Tiles_2.png' };
308	    const file = sheetByAnn[annKey];
309	    const tags = file ? this.atlas.annotations[file] : null;
310	    if (!tags) return null;
311	    // 惰性建组
312	    let groups = this.annGroups[annKey];
313	    if (!groups) {
314	      groups = {};
315	      for (const [name, tag] of Object.entries(tags)) {
316	        const rect = this.atlas.rect(file!, name);
317	        if (!rect) continue;
318	        (groups[tag] ??= []).push(rect);
319	      }
320	      this.annGroups[annKey] = groups;
321	    }
322	    if (!Object.keys(groups).length) return null;
323	    const sideState = (nx: number, ny: number): 'grass' | 'air' | 'other' => {
324	      if (!store.inBounds(nx, ny)) return 'air';
325	      const t = store.type[store.idx(nx, ny)];
326	      if (t === tileType) return 'grass';
327	      if (t === T_TREE) return 'air'; // 树干不是实心连接：草皮可贴着树干生长
328	      return t === 0 ? 'air' : 'other';
329	    };
330	    const T2 = sideState(x, y - 1), R2 = sideState(x + 1, y);
331	    const B2 = sideState(x, y + 1), L2 = sideState(x - 1, y);
332	    // 转角优先：一角两侧皆空气、且对角两侧有实心衔接 → 用角件过渡（地表线下降/上升的拐角）
333	    // tl: 上左皆空；tr: 上右皆空；bl: 下左皆空；br: 下右皆空
334	    const isAir = (s: string) => s === 'air';
335	    const hasSupport = (s1: string, s2: string) => s1 === 'other' || s2 === 'other' || s1 === 'grass' || s2 === 'grass';
336	    let cornerTag: string | null = null;
337	    if (isAir(T2) && isAir(L2) && hasSupport(B2, R2)) cornerTag = 'tl';
338	    else if (isAir(T2) && isAir(R2) && hasSupport(B2, L2)) cornerTag = 'tr';
339	    else if (isAir(B2) && isAir(L2) && hasSupport(T2, R2)) cornerTag = 'bl';
340	    else if (isAir(B2) && isAir(R2) && hasSupport(T2, L2)) cornerTag = 'br';
341	    // 兼容性评分选件（修"顶部长出底部/左下/右下件"的错位）：
342	    // 每件定义裸土侧（接泥土最佳）与草侧（朝空气最佳），按四邻状态打分取最低。
343	    // 索引：0上 1右 2下 3左
344	    const PIECES: Record<string, { bare: number[]; green: number[] }> = {
345	      top:    { bare: [2], green: [0, 1, 3] },
346	      bottom: { bare: [0], green: [2, 1, 3] },
347	      left:   { bare: [1], green: [3, 0, 2] },
348	      right:  { bare: [3], green: [1, 0, 2] },
349	      tl:     { bare: [1, 2], green: [0, 3] },
350	      tr:     { bare: [3, 2], green: [0, 1] },
351	      bl:     { bare: [1, 0], green: [2, 3] },
352	      br:     { bare: [3, 0], green: [2, 1] },
353	    };
354	    const sides = [T2, R2, B2, L2];
355	    const score = (tag: string): number => {
356	      if (tag === 'full') {
357	        return sides.reduce((acc, s) => acc + (s === 'grass' ? 0 : s === 'air' ? 1 : 2), 0);
358	      }
359	      const p = PIECES[tag];
360	      let sc = 0;
361	      for (const bi of p.bare) {
362	        const s = sides[bi];
363	        sc += s === 'other' ? 0 : s === 'grass' ? 1 : 2;
364	      }
365	      for (const gi of p.green) {
366	        const s = sides[gi];
367	        sc += s === 'air' ? 0 : s === 'grass' ? 1 : 3;
368	      }
369	      return sc;
370	    };
371	    let want: string, bestScore = Infinity;
372	    if (cornerTag && groups[cornerTag]?.length) {
373	      want = cornerTag; // 转角件优先
374	    } else {
375	      want = 'full';
376	      for (const tag of ['top', 'tl', 'tr', 'left', 'right', 'bl', 'br', 'bottom', 'full']) {
377	        if (!groups[tag]?.length) continue;
378	        const sc = score(tag);
379	        if (sc < bestScore) { bestScore = sc; want = tag; }
380	      }
381	    }
382	    const pickFrom = (tag: string): DrawRect | null => {
383	      const list = groups[tag];
384	      if (!list?.length) return null;
385	      return list[Math.floor(hash2(x, y, 551) * list.length) % list.length];
386	    };
387	    return pickFrom(want) ?? pickFrom('full') ?? (want !== 'top' ? pickFrom('top') : null);
388	  }
389	}
390	

```

```
64	const DEFAULT_FRAME: Frame = [1, 1];
65	
66	/** 8 向邻居掩码（vanilla 位序：U=1 L=2 R=4 D=8 UL=16 UR=32 DL=64 DR=128） */
67	export function mask8(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number, same: (t: number) => boolean): number {
68	  const at = (ax: number, ay: number) => st.inBounds(ax, ay) && same(st.type[st.idx(ax, ay)]);
69	  let m = 0;
70	  if (at(x, y - 1)) m |= 1;    // U
71	  if (at(x - 1, y)) m |= 2;    // L
72	  if (at(x + 1, y)) m |= 4;    // R
73	  if (at(x, y + 1)) m |= 8;    // D
74	  // 角：两相邻正交邻也在时角才计入（vanilla BlockStyle 语义的简化）
75	  if ((m & 3) === 3 && at(x - 1, y - 1)) m |= 16;    // UL
76	  if ((m & 5) === 5 && at(x + 1, y - 1)) m |= 32;    // UR
77	  if ((m & 10) === 10 && at(x - 1, y + 1)) m |= 64;  // DL
78	  if ((m & 12) === 12 && at(x + 1, y + 1)) m |= 128; // DR
79	  return m;
80	}
81	
82	/** auto 帧格的实际渲染帧（col,row）——与渲染端同一查找表，供纠错面板定位 */
83	export function autoFrameAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number): { col: number; row: number } {
84	  const entry = LOOKUP[mask8(st, x, y, type, (t) => t === type)];
85	  if (!entry) return { col: DEFAULT_FRAME[0], row: DEFAULT_FRAME[1] };
86	  const v = ((x * 7) + (y * 11)) % 3 % entry.length;
87	  return { col: entry[v][0], row: entry[v][1] };
88	}
89	
90	/** 'auto' 帧：查表 + 变体。返回 (col,row) 或 null（回退默认帧由调用方处理） */
91	export function frameFor(atlas: SpriteAtlas, sheet: number, st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number, same: (t: number) => boolean): DrawRect | null {
92	  const entry = LOOKUP[mask8(st, x, y, type, same)];
93	  let r: DrawRect | null = null;
94	  if (entry) {
95	    const v = entry[((x * 7) + (y * 11)) % 3 % entry.length];
96	    r = atlas.vframe(sheet, v[0], v[1]);
97	    if (r && !frameHasContent(r)) r = null; // 空帧（如草皮表缺 13-17 列的边缘帧）→ 回退
98	  }
99	  if (!r) r = atlas.vframe(sheet, DEFAULT_FRAME[0], DEFAULT_FRAME[1]);
100	  return r;
101	}
102	
103	/** auto 帧内容检测缓存：key = 图像:像素偏移。查找表引用的帧在部分表（草皮族等
104	 *  窄表）上是空像素，直画会整格空白 → 检出后回退默认帧 */
105	const frameContentCache = new Map<string, boolean>();
106	function frameHasContent(r: DrawRect): boolean {
107	  const key = `${r.img.width}x${r.img.height}:${r.sx},${r.sy}`;
108	  const hit = frameContentCache.get(key);
109	  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
110	  let ok = false;
111	  try {
112	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
113	    c.width = r.sw; c.height = r.sh;
114	    const cx = c.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true })!;
115	    cx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, 0, 0, r.sw, r.sh);
116	    const d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, r.sw, r.sh).data;
117	    let n = 0;
118	    for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 16) { if (d[i] > 40 && ++n >= 4) { ok = true; break; } }
119	  } catch { ok = true; /* 采样失败按有内容处理 */ }
120	  frameContentCache.set(key, ok);
121	  return ok;
122	}
123	
124	/** 树木专用渲染（tile 5，移植 TEdit WorldRenderXna 树逻辑）：
125	 *  - 树干帧（frameY < 198）：按树下草地生物群系选 Tiles_5_0..6 变体，取 (U,V,20,20) 压入 16px 格
126	 *  - 标记帧（frameX >= 22 且 frameY >= 198）：不采样 Tiles_5——
127	 *    U=22 树顶（Tree_Tops_style 80×80 底部锚定）、U=44 左枝（Tree_Branches 40×40 右锚定）、
128	 *    U=66 右枝（左锚定）；变体 x%3
129	 *  treeStyle 固定 0（wld 树样式段未解析，默认森林） */
130	// 树类 tile → Tree_Tops/Branches 样式（TEdit：5 森林 0 / 宝石树 583-589 → 22-28 /
131	// 装饰树 596 樱花→29、616 柳→30 / 灰烬树 634 → 31）
132	const TREE_SHEET_STYLES: Record<number, number> = { 5: 0, 596: 29, 616: 30, 634: 31 };
133	for (let i = 583; i <= 589; i++) TREE_SHEET_STYLES[i] = 22 + (i - 583);
134	/** 树冠帧尺寸按 PNG 实际宽度推导（3 变体等分 + 2px 间隔；高度 -2）：
135	 *  自动覆盖全部样式（森林 80×80 / 丛林·宝石 114-116×96 / 装饰 118×96 / 神圣 80-114×140） */
136	function treeTopSize(img: HTMLImageElement): [number, number] {
137	  return [Math.floor(img.width / 3) - 2, img.height - 2];
138	}
139	// 树下草地 → 生物群系树干变体（TEdit treeType；-1 = 基础 Tiles_5）
140	const TREE_GROUND: Array<[string, number]> = [
141	  ['v_23_corrupt_grass_block', 0],
142	  ['v_60_jungle_grass_block', 1],
143	  ['v_109_hallowed_grass_block', 2],
144	  ['snow', 3],
145	  ['v_199_crimson_grass_block', 4],
146	  ['v_70_mushroom_grass_block', 6],
147	];
148	/** 原版 WorldGen.GetCommonTreeFoliageData 样式选择移植（WorldGen.cs:37381-37496）。
149	 *  trees = { treeX, treeStyle(header), treeTops(v≥211 权威 13 变体), worldSurface, worldW }
150	 *  变体来源优先 treeTops（GetTreeStyle(areaId)），空表回退 header treeStyle[0..3] */
151	function areaStyle(trees: TreeStyleData, areaId: number): number {
152	  if (trees.treeTops?.length) return trees.treeTops[areaId] ?? 0;
153	  return trees.treeStyle?.[areaId] ?? 0;
154	}
155	
156	export function crownStyleFor(x: number, treeType: number, floorY: number, trees: TreeStyleData): number {
157	  const gs = (id: number) => areaStyle(trees, id);
158	  switch (treeType) {
159	    case 0: return 1;   // 23 腐化草
160	    case 1: {           // 60 丛林草：v[5]==1 → 11；深处 → 13（覆盖）；否则 2
161	      let st = gs(5) === 1 ? 11 : 2;
162	      if (floorY > (trees.worldSurface ?? 0)) st = 13;
163	      return st;
164	    }
165	    case 2: {           // 109/492 神圣草：hallowBG 未解析 → 固定 3
166	      return 3;
167	    }
168	    case 3: {           // 147 雪原：完整 case-147 逻辑
169	      let st = 4;
170	      const v6 = gs(6);
171	      if (v6 === 0) { st = 12; if (x % 10 === 0) st = 18; }
172	      if (v6 === 2 || v6 === 3 || v6 === 32 || v6 === 4 || v6 === 42 || v6 === 5 || v6 === 7) {
173	        st = v6 % 2 !== 0
174	          ? (x <= (trees.worldW ?? 0) / 2 ? 17 : 16)
175	          : (x >= (trees.worldW ?? 0) / 2 ? 17 : 16);
176	      }
177	      return st;
178	    }
179	    case 4: return 5;   // 199 猩红草
180	    case 6: return 14;  // 70 蘑菇草
181	    default: {          // 2/477 普通草：四区 + 0→0/5→10/其余 5+s
182	      const num = x > (trees.treeX?.[0] ?? 0) ? (x > (trees.treeX?.[1] ?? 0) ? (x > (trees.treeX?.[2] ?? 0) ? gs(3) : gs(2)) : gs(1)) : gs(0);
183	      if (num === 0) return 0;
184	      return num === 5 ? 10 : 5 + num;
185	    }
186	  }
187	}

189	/** 向下扫描树下的草地定生物群系树型（原版 GetCommonTreeFoliageData 扫描）。
190	 *  返回 treeType（-1 普通草/森林）与发现草地的深度 floorY（丛林深处判定用） */
191	export function scanTreeType(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): { type: number; floorY: number } {
192	  let type = -1;
193	  let floorY = y;
194	  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
195	    const ty = y + i;
196	    if (ty >= st.h) break;
197	    floorY = ty;
198	    const t = st.type[st.idx(x, ty)];
199	    if (t === 0) continue;
200	    if (t === TILE_BY_KEY['grass']) { type = -1; break; }
201	    const hit = TREE_GROUND.find(([k]) => TILE_BY_KEY[k] === t);
202	    if (hit) { type = hit[1]; break; }
203	  }
204	  return { type, floorY };
205	}

208	  treeX: number[];
209	  treeStyle: number[];
210	  treeTops?: number[];
211	  worldSurface?: number;
212	  worldW?: number;
213	}

215	export function drawTreeCell(
216	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas,
217	  st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, fx: number, fy: number, px: number, py: number,
218	  sheet = 5,
219	  foliageOnly = false,
220	  trees?: TreeStyleData,
221	): void {
222	  const style = TREE_SHEET_STYLES[sheet] ?? 0;
223	  // 棕榈树（tile 323）：树冠帧藏在树干列内（frameX 88-132，无独立标记格）。
224	  //  TEdit：Tree_Tops_15（80×80、82px 步长），srcX = 变体×82、srcY = 沙型×82，
225	  //  底部锚定 + X 偏移 frameY/16 格；沙型 = 下方沙块种类（普通0/猩红1/珍珠2/腐化3）
226	  if (sheet === 323) {
227	    if (fx >= 88 && fx <= 132) {
228	      if (!foliageOnly) return; // 树冠/干身全部由外扩遍绘制（倾斜跨列/跨 chunk 防裁剪）
229	      const img15 = atlas.vimages.get('vanilla/Tree_Tops_15.png');
230	      if (!img15) return;
231	      // 沙型：向下扫沙块
232	      let palmType = 0;
233	      for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
234	        const ty = y + i;
235	        if (ty >= st.h) break;
236	        const t = st.type[st.idx(x, ty)];
237	        if (t === 0) continue;
238	        const tk = TILE_DEFS[t]?.key;
239	        palmType = tk === 'v_234_crimsand_block' ? 1 : tk === 'v_116_pearlsand_block' ? 2
240	          : tk === 'v_112_ebonsand_block' ? 3 : 0;
241	        break;
242	      }
243	      const v323 = Math.floor((fx - 88) / 22); // 0/1/2 变体
244	      const sx15 = v323 * 82, sy15 = palmType * 82;
245	      if (sx15 + 80 > img15.width || sy15 + 80 > img15.height) return;
246	      // 底部锚定居中 + X 偏移 = frameY【像素·有符号】（原版 dest.X += V*zoom/16，
247	      // zoom=16 即 V 像素；65534 = -2 的 u16 回绕）
248	      const leanCrown = fy >= 32768 ? fy - 65536 : fy;
249	      ctx.drawImage(img15, sx15, sy15, 80, 80,
250	        px + (TILE - 80) / 2 + leanCrown, py + TILE - 80, 80, 80);
251	      return;
252	    }
253	    // 干身（原版 TileDrawing case 323：20×20 帧 22px 步长，底部对齐；
254	    // frameY 是**有符号像素偏移**——棕榈树干随高度倾斜，65534 = -2 的 u16 回绕）
255	    const img323 = atlas.vimages.get('vanilla/Tiles_323.png');
256	    if (!img323 || fx + 20 > img323.width) return;
257	    const lean = fy >= 32768 ? fy - 65536 : fy; // 原版 vector2.X += frameY
258	    const pad323 = treeFrameBottomPad(img323, fx, 0);
259	    const artH323 = 20 - pad323;
260	    ctx.drawImage(img323, fx, 0, 20, 20, px - 2 + lean, py + TILE - artH323, 20, 20);
261	    return;
262	  }
263	  // 蘑菇树（tile 72）：独立渲染路径——U≥36 标记帧 = 巨蘑菇顶（Shroom_Tops，60×42、62px 步长、底部锚定）
264	  if (sheet === 72) {
265	    if (foliageOnly === false && fx >= 36) return; // 顶格由外扩遍画
266	    if (fx >= 36) {
267	      const img72 = atlas.vimages.get('vanilla/Shroom_Tops.png');
268	      if (img72 && fx + 60 <= img72.width && fy + 42 <= img72.height) {
269	        // TEdit：source.X = (V/18)*62；60×42 底部锚定居中
270	        const sx72 = Math.floor(fy / 18) * 62;
271	        if (sx72 + 60 <= img72.width) {
272	          ctx.drawImage(img72, sx72, 0, 60, 42, px + (TILE - 60) / 2, py + TILE - 42, 60, 42);
273	        }
274	      }
275	      return;
276	    }
277	    // 干身：Tiles_72 直取（54×54 表，2×2 帧 18px 网格）
278	    const img72 = atlas.vimages.get('vanilla/Tiles_72.png');
279	    if (img72 && fx + 16 <= img72.width && fy + 16 <= img72.height) {
280	      ctx.drawImage(img72, fx, fy, 16, 16, px, py, TILE, TILE);
281	    }
282	    return;
283	  }
284	  if (fy >= 198 && fx >= 22) {
285	    // 树冠/树枝标记帧（仅由 ChunkCache 的外扩遍绘制，防跨 chunk 裁剪）
286	    if (foliageOnly === false) return;
287	    // 变体来源 = 标记帧自身 frameY（原版 WorldGen.GetTreeFrame：198→0/220→1/242→2）

289	    // 样式选择（原版 GetCommonTreeFoliageData；树族 tile 用固定样式映射）
290	    // 树型扫描列：左枝在右侧树干列找地 / 右枝在左侧（原版 xoffset）
291	    const scanX = x + (fx === 44 ? 1 : fx === 66 ? -1 : 0);
292	    const scan = scanTreeType(st, scanX, y);
293	    const crown = sheet === 5 && trees
294	      ? crownStyleFor(scanX, scan.type, scan.floorY, trees)
295	      : style;
296	    let img: HTMLImageElement | null;
297	    let sx = 0, sy = 0, sw = 0, sh = 0, anchor = 0; // 0=底中(顶) 1=右中(左枝) 2=左中(右枝)
298	    if (fx === 22) {
299	      img = atlas.vimages.get(`vanilla/Tree_Tops_${crown}.png`)
300	        ?? atlas.vimages.get(`vanilla/Tree_Tops_${style}.png`) ?? null;
```

```
1. Where particles/dmgNumbers are managed (Game.ts) - caps and cleanup.
2. ChunkCache.flushDirty call frequency and chunks.get - the `chunks.set(k, undefined)` + re-get pattern. Note: `markDirty` sets chunk to undefined and pushes to dirtyQueue, but `dirtyQueue.includes(k)` is O(n) and dirtyQueue can grow unbounded? Also chunks Map is unbounded — no eviction. Chunks created lazily and never removed → unbounded growth across exploration (each chunk = 2 canvases of 16*16=256px each = 256*256*4 bytes = 256KB each canvas, 512KB per chunk pair). That's a big leak-ish growth. World size? Let me check constants. A large world 4200x1200 tiles → chunks 263x75 = ~19700 chunks → 19700 * 512KB = 10GB! But only explored ones created. Still unbounded.

3. Minimap constructor registers onTileChanged listener; Minimap is created per world? If new world loaded, new Minimap created and old listeners may not be unregistered → listener leak. Need to check TileStore.onTileChanged implementation.

5. In Minimap.flushDirty - it iterates dirtyChunks while deleting, capped at 25 per frame. OK. But `redrawAll` is O(world.w*world.h) full map scan - triggered at construction and maybe on world load. Also colorFor does parseInt on hex string per tile — peak type.

6. Minimap canvas is world.w × world.h pixels — for a large world 4200x1200 = 5M pixels = 20MB. That's a one-time allocation.

Let me check Game.ts for particle caps, render loop, minimap creation, chunk flush.
```

```
Key findings so far:

1. TileStore.listeners: no unsubscribe API. ChunkCache, LightingEngine, Minimap, LiquidSim all register listeners on the World's store. If a new world is loaded (Game.ts line 320-330: `this.chunks = new ChunkCache(w...)`, `this.lighting = new LightingEngine(w)`, `this.renderer.minimap = new Minimap(w)`) — need to check whether `w` is a new World each time (new store) or the same store. If new World per load, old listeners GC with old store — fine. If same store, leak. Let me check Game.ts around line 300-340.

2. Note LiquidSim constructor registers onTileChanged and onLiquidChanged. Also each `new LiquidSim(world)` (e.g. "预沉降临时实例" mentioned in dispose comment) — check if quickSettle creates temp LiquidSim instances repeatedly. dispose() sets disposed=true to stop responding, but the listener remains in the array forever (holding the sim + its checking/skip arrays of w*h bytes each = 2 arrays × world size). If a temp sim is created per save load, that's a leak of w*h*2 bytes per instance. Let me grep for `new LiquidSim`.

3. Minimap registers onTileChanged in constructor. Game.ts line 330 `this.renderer.minimap = new Minimap(w)` — each new world replaces minimap; if the store is new each time, fine; if the store persists... check.

Also particles: capped at 600 via splice (line 2529). dmgNumbers filtered each tick (line 745) — new array per tick via filter: `this.particles = this.particles.filter(...)` at 739 and dmgNumbers at 745 — two array allocations per tick (60/s). Small but note. dmgNumbers has no cap — but they expire (life decrements). Let me check lines 710-750 of Game.ts to confirm life decrement. Also 2416 filter.

4. In Renderer.render: `const sorted = [...entities].sort(...)` — new array + sort every frame. Fine-ish, GC pressure small.

5. compositeLight: `lc.createImageData(w2,h2)` every frame — allocation of w2*h2*4 bytes. For 1920x1080 at zoom 1.25: tilesX = ceil(1920/1.25/16)+2 = 98, tilesY=ceil(1080/1.25/16)+2=56. w2=196, h2=112 → 196*112*4 = 87,808 bytes ≈ 86KB per frame. Plus tap() returns tuple arrays `[r,g,b]` — 4 allocations per pixel! tap is called 4 times per pixel, each returns a new array of 3 numbers → 4 arrays/px × 21952 px = 87,808 small arrays per frame. That's a major GC pressure point. Confirmed at lines 1204-1221.

6. VanillaLiquidRenderer: allocations per call: Float32Array×6 (level, visLevel, opacity, lW, rW, bW, tW — let me count: level, visLevel, opacity (3), lW,rW,bW,tW (4), vlW,vrW,vbW,vtW (4) = 11 Float32Arrays), Uint8Array×9 (isSolidA, hasLiquidA, hasWallA, hasVisA, typeA, visTypeA, hasLE, hasRE, hasTE, hasBE, isHalfA = 11), Int16Array×2 (fx, fy). Total 24 typed arrays per call. Called twice per frame (background + foreground pass). n = pw*ph. For 1920x1080 zoom... view tiles ~ (1920/1.25/16+4)*(1080/1.25/16+~10) ≈ 98*62 ≈ 6076 cells. Float32: 11*4 bytes*n = 44B/cell; Uint8 11*1 = 11B/cell; Int16 2*2=4B/cell. Total ~59B/cell × 6076 ≈ 358KB per pass, ×2 passes = ~717KB/frame ≈ 43MB/s at 60fps. Plus per-call texCache Map allocation (1 Map + closures texFor/at per call ×2). Yes: `const at = (lx,ly) =>` closure per call, `texCache` Map per call, `texFor` closure per call. 2 calls/frame.

Also note: no cross-frame cache — comment says "每帧分配，视图 ~5000 格，量级可控". Actually it's per pass, so double.

7. ChunkCache: 
- chunks Map unbounded, no eviction. Each chunk = 2 canvases 256×256 = 2×256KB = 512KB (RGBA). For a large world explored fully: (4200/16)×(1200/16) = 262×75 = 19,687 chunks × 512KB ≈ 9.8GB worst case. Even partial exploration, canvas memory dominates. Unbounded growth — leak-type.
- markDirty: `this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)` O(n) — with shift() from queue making O(n²) behavior. dirtyQueue length unbounded? Entries only removed in flushDirty (max 4/frame via shift — O(n) each). During world gen or massive edits (explosives), queue can grow to thousands; shift() on array is O(n). Also `chunks.set(k, undefined)` then get() re-renders.
- Note in flushDirty: `if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue;` — but chunks.set(k, undefined) marks deletion... a chunk never rendered (not in map) returns undefined from get → would re-render via this.get(cx,cy). Hmm, `markDirty` only enqueues if chunks.has(k). OK.
- Also `markDirtyAround` is registered via onTileChanged → every setTile marks up to 3 chunks (self + edge neighbors). During world gen with silent writes it's bypassed.
- renderChunk: allocates 2 canvases per chunk rebuild; the treeIds array `.map().filter()` is rebuilt per chunk render (line 266-270) — allocation per chunk rebuild, minor. Also `{ treeX... }` object literal per drawVanillaCell call (line 183-184) — per tile per chunk rebuild! That's an object per tile.

8. WaterfallRenderer: 
- findWaterfalls every 30 frames scans (tx1-tx0+200)×(ty1-ty0+120) ≈ (98+200)×(56+120) ≈ 298×176 ≈ 52,448 cells per scan (~2Hz). No allocation except falls array reset + push objects (up to 1000 Waterfall objects). `this.falls.length = 0` reuses array but pushes new object literals each scan (1000 objects per scan worst case). Minor.
- draw() every frame: `SHEET.map(...)` allocates a new array + 3 closures... actually `const solid = (x,y) => ...` closure defined inside findWaterfalls each scan, and in draw: `const solidT = (x,y) => {...}` defined inside the per-waterfall loop! Line 90: inside `for (const wf of this.falls)` — so one closure allocation per waterfall per frame (up to 1000 closures/frame). That's GC pressure. Plus `tex` array per frame (SHEET.map = 1 array + 3 elements/frame). Each waterfall walks up to 100 steps → 1000 falls × 100 steps = 100k drawImage worst case per frame — but realistic falls limited.
- Comment in Renderer.drawWaterfalls says "扫描窗口外扩 100 格在内部，30 帧节流" — confirmed.

9. LightingEngine:
- compute(): `new Int32Array(rw*rh*4)` every recompute! rw*rh for 1920×1080@1.25 zoom + margin 26: rw ≈ 98+52=150, rh ≈ 56+52=108 → 16,200 cells × 4 × 4 bytes = 259KB per recompute. Recompute happens when: dirty (tile change) — every tile change triggers full recompute next frame; liquidDirty throttled to 20Hz (so up to 20 times/sec = 5.2MB/s); camera moved > 2 tiles (continuous while walking → every frame while moving!). So while walking + liquid flowing: allocation each frame ~259KB + full BFS. That's big.
- Also lightAt returns tuple; BFS loop itself no alloc.
- ensureRegion only reallocs r/g/b on resize.

10. LiquidSim:
- Not full-map per tick: processes slice = curMaxLiquid/cycles = 25000/7 ≈ 3571 entries per call... wait: slice = floor(25000/7) = 3571; start = slice*(wetCounter-1). But numLiquid may be far less; `if (end > numLiquid) {end = numLiquid; wetCounter = cycles}`. So it processes min(slice, numLiquid) entries per step. step() called every 2 ticks. So bounded by active list, not full map. Good.
- addWater allocates `{x,y,kill,delay}` object literal per entry (line 114) — up to 25000 live entries; each wake allocates a new object while the array slot is reused... actually `this.liquids[this.numLiquid] = {...}` new object per addWater. During heavy flow, thousands of object allocations per tick. GC pressure moderate. Note swap-remove in delWater copies fields, objects retained in array (length trimmed). Dense array, OK.
- update(): `for (const [nx,ny] of [[x-1,y],...] as const)` — allocates an array + iterator + destructured arrays per water cell per tick! Two such loops (lava wake at line 213, honey wake at 226) per water cell update. Each: outer array literal (1), iterator object, and per-iteration destructuring creates... `[nx,ny]` destructuring from const array — the inner arrays are pre-created in the literal (4 sub-arrays). So per update: 2 × (1 outer array + 4 inner arrays + iterator + ...) ≈ 10-12 objects per cell per tick. With ~3500 cells processed per step, 2 steps... step called every 2 ticks → at 60fps that's 30 steps/s → 3500×12×30 ≈ 1.26M objects/s during active water flow. Significant GC pressure. This is a real finding: LiquidSim.ts:213 and 226.
- buffer: `Array<{x,y}>` capped 49998, uses shift() (O(n)) — when full and draining, O(n²). Peak scenario.
- Also onTileChanged in constructor: for each tile change it calls addWater 9 times (3×3). During mining this is fine, but note listeners never removed.

- The `checking`/`skip` Uint8Arrays: w*h each = for 4200×1200 = 5M bytes each ×2 = 10MB per LiquidSim instance. If temp instances created for pre-settle (dispose), these stay alive as long as the listener holds a reference! LiquidSim registers listener in constructor; dispose() only sets flag — the closure remains in store.listeners forever → checking + skip arrays (10MB for large world) leaked per disposed instance. Need to check how temp instances are used. grep "new LiquidSim".

11. Minimap:
- canvas = world.w × world.h: 4200×1200 = 5.04M px = 20MB one-time. Plus redrawAll createImageData another 20MB transient.
- redrawAll full scan with parseInt per cell — peak (world load / minimap creation).
- flushDirty: capped 24 chunks/frame, uses per-pixel fillRect (256 fillRects per chunk → 6144 fillRect calls/frame max) — CPU heavy but bounded. Also `colorFor` returns strings — fillStyle string assignment per pixel; no alloc besides.
- onTileChanged listener adds to dirtyChunks set — Set<number> unbounded? Entries removed in flushDirty up to 24/frame. During liquid... no, only tile changes. During explosive/gen events could grow but bounded by chunk count. Note: iterating a Set while deleting inside for...of is OK.
- Full map draw: drawFullMap uses minimap canvas scaled — fine.

- Minimap created per world at Game.ts:330. Check whether old minimap's listener on old store is discarded. If a new World object each load → old store GC'd. Check Game.ts loadWorld.

12. Renderer.render allocations per frame:
- `const sorted = [...entities].sort(...)` — 1 array/frame.
- compositeLight: createImageData + 4 tap tuples/pixel (huge).
- drawGrandPreview: cells array per frame when dragging (small).
- drawMinimap: minimapRect object literal per frame (1), timeText strings per frame (several small strings: padStart etc).
- drawFullMap: `this.lastMouse = {x,y}` object per frame when map open (1).
- drawDebugOverlay: box/circle closures per frame + template strings (only F3).
- dmgNumbers: `String(d.value)` per number per frame.
- drawHp: no alloc besides closure inHeart/outline defined inside drawPixelHeart — those are closures created per heart per frame! drawPixelHeart defines `inHeart` and `outline` arrow functions per call; called up to 6×/frame → 12 closures/frame. Minor.

13. VanillaTiler frameFor: creates closure `same` passed from ChunkCache per tile (line 181: `(t) => t === type` — arrow closure per tile per chunk render). mask8's `at` closure per call. These are during chunk rebuild (4/frame max) — 256 tiles × few closures each = ~1-2K closures per rebuild, minor steady state, but during initial world load / camera pan, many chunks built → thousands.

Also drawVanillaCell passes a fresh object literal `{treeX..., worldW}` per tile (ChunkCache.ts:183-185) — allocation per tile per chunk rebuild.
```

```
Key findings on world load:

- `settleLiquids` creates a temp LiquidSim (`new LiquidSim(this.world)`) which registers listeners on `this.world.store`. `sim.dispose()` only sets `disposed = true` — the comment says "注销监听" but dispose() does NOT remove the listener from store.listeners! There's no unsubscribe API in TileStore. So every world load leaks a LiquidSim closure holding `checking` + `skip` Uint8Arrays (2 × w × h bytes). For a large world 4200×1200 = 5.04M cells → 10MB leaked per world load. And each subsequent setTile fires the dead listener (cheap no-op due to disposed check, but still iterates). That's a genuine leak — repeated load/import grows unboundedly.

Also afterWorldLoad: `new ChunkCache(w...)`, `new LightingEngine(w)`, `new LiquidSim(w)`, `new Minimap(w)` each register listeners on the SAME store (w = this.world, which may be a new World object per load — check loadWorld: `loadWorld(world)` takes world param, presumably a fresh World). If each load creates a fresh World with a fresh store, then old listeners die with the old store. But within one session, if afterWorldLoad is called multiple times on the same world object (is it?), listeners accumulate. Need to check whether loadWorld is only called once per World instance. Probably. But settleLiquids temp sim leaks within the same store as the persistent LiquidSim → definite leak per load (1 temp sim per load). Also if world regenerate happens repeatedly (returning to menu and generating new worlds), old Worlds presumably GC'd. But wait — does anything hold a reference to old World? `this.renderer.minimap` is replaced; old minimap holds old world — fine if no other refs.

Actually there's a subtlety: Game.loadWorld is called per world; each new World → new store → listeners fine. The temp LiquidSim in settleLiquids leaks relative to that store: one per world, holding 2×w×h bytes for the lifetime of that store (which is the world session). So it's a constant 10MB overhead per session rather than unbounded growth — unless loadWorld is called repeatedly on the same world (re-import?). Also `import` path may call loadWorld again on same world → accumulates.

Let me check how loadWorld/import works quickly. Also check Game.ts render() around line 2709 and the particle update 700-750. And check if there are other per-frame items. Let me also verify the store.setTile listeners count: each setTile iterates all listeners (4-5 listeners now: ChunkCache.markDirtyAround, LightingEngine.rebuildSkyColumn+dirty, LiquidSim temp (disposed), LiquidSim live, Minimap dirtyChunks).
```
